Polymer cards and original evidence

Polymers are retained because they are identified as polymers. Performance, purchase status and missing card fields do not decide retention.

Open each “Original evidence” section to see the source text and location. A matched quotation does not by itself verify the scientific interpretation.

Polymer identification

The source explicitly states that PTMG-HDI was synthesized and presents it as one of the poly(ether-urethane) polymer structures.

Unresolved source discrepancy

thickness: The source reports different SHPEM/SHP membrane thickness values for different described forms or test contexts: 12 μm in Methods electrochemical tests, about 15 μm for electrochemical performance tests, ~20 μm for a prepared membrane with 10% mass solid electrolyte loading, and about 25 μm in the healing demonstration caption. Their relationship is not fully resolved as one identical specimen.

Reported specimens: All the SHP electrolyte membranes used in electrochemical performance tests, prepared SHP electrolyte membrane (~20 μm thick) with 10% mass solid electrolyte loading, SHP electrolyte membranes (the thickness of the membranes is about 25 μm)

    5. main · Methods / Polymer synthesis, paragraph 29 · chunk src-abdf1ced28f9b1e9fb83

      HO–PDMS–OH (23.0 g, 5.0 mmol) in a dried glass vessel equipped with a mechanical stirrer was heated in an oil bath at 100 °C under vacuum (<133 Pa) for 1 h to remove any moisture, and then cooled to 70 °C. IPDI (2.44 g, 11 mmol) and DBTDL (0.08 g) were dissolved in DMAc (20 mL), dropwise added into the vessel, and stirred for 3 h under a N₂ atmosphere. After the synthesis of the pre-polymer, 4,4^{′}-dithiodianiline (0.6 g, 2.5 mmol) and 4, 4^{′}-bis(hydroxymethyl)-2,2^{′}-bipyridine (0.465 g, 2.5 mmol) in 6 mL of dry DMAc were added to the pre-polymer solution. After stirring at 70 °C for another 3 h, the solution was added by methanol (3 mL), and further stirred for 30 min to ensure the consumption enough of isocyanate groups.

  • P3 (1.00 g) was dissolved in 6 mL of CH₂Cl₂. Subsequently, the solution was ultrasonicated to remove bubbles and poured into a PTFE dish (diameter 3.5 cm) that was covered with a glass petri dish, slowly evaporated at room temperature overnight. The resulting film was turned over and dried again for 8 h. Afterward, the film was dried at 65 °C in a vacuum oven for 12 h. After drying, the film was cut into small pieces for further testing.

    Tested/prepared specimen: P3 film

    Original evidence (1 source passage)

    1. main · Methods / Film preparation for self-healing and mechanical property testing, paragraph 30 · chunk src-3c26b5356cd78c6d5409

      P3 (1.00 g) was dissolved in 6 mL of CH₂Cl₂. Subsequently, the solution was ultrasonicated to remove bubbles and poured into a PTFE dish (diameter 3.5 cm) that was covered with a glass petri dish, slowly evaporated at room temperature overnight. The resulting film was turned over and dried again for 8 h. Afterward, the film was dried at 65 °C in a vacuum oven for 12 h. After drying, the film was cut into small pieces for further testing. The same procedure was used to prepare films based on other ratios (PXs).

      Selected source passage retrieved exactly from converted source text

      Passage selection: spn-5cdebad2c3f4193294033c31039e89d8. Source characters [0, 515) in the unchanged chunk (Unicode character offsets).

The round polymer tube was prepared with 36 mm long, 1.5 mm thick, and 2 mm inside diameter.

      P3 (1.00 g) was dissolved in 6 mL of CH₂Cl₂. Subsequently, the solution was ultrasonicated to remove bubbles and poured into a PTFE dish (diameter 3.5 cm) that was covered with a glass petri dish, slowly evaporated at room temperature overnight. The resulting film was turned over and dried again for 8 h. Afterward, the film was dried at 65 °C in a vacuum oven for 12 h. After drying, the film was cut into small pieces for further testing. The same procedure was used to prepare films based on other ratios (PXs).

      P3 (1.00 g) was dissolved in 6 mL of CH₂Cl₂. Subsequently, the solution was ultrasonicated to remove bubbles and poured into a PTFE dish (diameter 3.5 cm) that was covered with a glass petri dish, slowly evaporated at room temperature overnight. The resulting film was turned over and dried again for 8 h. Afterward, the film was dried at 65 °C in a vacuum oven for 12 h. After drying, the film was cut into small pieces for further testing. The same procedure was used to prepare films based on other ratios (PXs).
